          +e master context plus one prompt per capability ("increment").
        5 +**MANDATORY:** Do the following now, and nothing else:        
        6 +1. Save this entire prompt **verbatim** to `prompts/00_master_
          +context.md`.                                                  
        7 +2. Create `CLAUDE.md` in the repo root. It must say: "Before a
          +ny work, read `prompts/00_master_context.md` and `docs/PROGRES
          +S.md`. Follow the Working Rules in the master context." Copy S
          +ections 7 and 8 of this prompt into it as well.               
        8 +3. Create `docs/PROGRESS.md` with the increment table from Sec
          +tion 9 (all rows "Not started").                              
        9 +4. Create `docs/DECISIONS.md` (empty log, with a heading).    
       10 +5. **Do NOT write any application code yet.** Increment 01 com
          +es in the next prompt.                                        
       11 +6. Reply with a short confirmation and the file list.         
       12 +                                                              
       13 +---                                                           
       14 +                                                              
       15 +## 1. Domain background                                       
       16 +                                                              
       17 +Applications call external REST APIs. Providers change their *
          +*OpenAPI** specs: endpoints get removed, fields get renamed, t
          +ypes change, and new required parameters are added. Today a de
          +veloper has to notice the change, find the affected code, fix 
          +it, and prove the fix works. Existing tools cover only one pie
          +ce of that:                                                   
       18 +- Dependabot/Renovate bump versions but don't change code.    
       19 +- OpenRewrite/ast-grep need a hand-written rule.              
       20 +- Spec-diff tools report changes but don't fix consumer code. 
       21 +                                                              
       22 +**Our system joins the whole path** (spec history → validated 
          +fix in the consumer's code → pull request) **and measures** ho
          +w often an LLM agent repairs a breaking change correctly, and 
          +where it fails.                                               
       23 +                                                              
       24 +The full requirements are in `docs/SRS.pdf` (Group 13 SRS v1.0
          +). **Requirement IDs (FR-xx, NFR-xx, DR-xx, BR-xx, C-xx, EV-xx
          +, AT-xx) refer to that document.** When a prompt cites an ID, 
          +read that part of the SRS.                                    
       25 +                                                              
       26 +## 2. System architecture — 7-stage pipeline                  
       27 +                                                              
       28 +Each stage **writes its result to the database before the next
          + stage begins** (FR-38). Runs are **append-only** (FR-39).    
       29 +                                                              
       30 +| # | Stage | Input | Output recorded |                       
       31 +|---|-------|-------|-----------------|                       
       32 +| 1 | Ingest | Spec URL or uploaded file | Spec version, norma
          +lized JSON, SHA-256 hash |                                    
       33 +| 2 | Detect | Two spec versions | Structured diff list (path,
          + method, JSON pointer, before/after evidence) |               
       34 +| 3 | Classify | One difference | breaking / safe / unknown, c
          +onfidence 0–1, mapping status |                               
       35 +| 4 | Locate | Change + repo | Call sites (file, line, column,
          + pattern, excerpt) + coverage report |                        
       36 +| 5 | Repair | Change, sites, migration note, prior errors | P
          +atch (unified diff), tokens, cost, model, attempt # |         
       37 +| 6 | Validate | Patch + isolated working copy | pass / fail /
          + cheating (+ guard name) |                                    
       38 +| 7 | Deliver | Validated patch | PR URL, or flagged finding +
          + reason |                                                     
       39 +                                                              
       40 +**THE RULE THAT IS NEVER BROKEN (BR-04, C-04):** A spec differ
          +ence is NOT proof that two fields mean the same thing. Stage 5
          + (Repair) runs **only** when the old→new mapping is **confirme
          +d by a migration note or a human**. Everything else stops at s
          +tage 4 and is reported.                                       
     … 106 more lines (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Write(README.md)
  ⎿  Wrote 49 lines to README.md
      1 # Self-Maintaining APIs
      2 
      3 When a REST API provider changes its OpenAPI spec in a breaking w
        ay, this system detects the change, finds the affected call sites
         in a TypeScript consumer repo, asks an LLM agent for a fix, vali
        dates that fix in an isolated Docker sandbox, and opens a pull re
        quest for human review. It also measures how often the agent repa
        irs breaking changes correctly, and where it fails. (CS 4398, Gro
        up 13. Requirements: `docs/SRS.pdf`.)
      4

      27 +export interface SpecDifference {                              
      28 +  id: string;              // stable: sha256(ruleId|method|path

         +roperty-type-changed"

         +cument for the changed item                                    
      34 +  description: string;     // oasdiff's text                   
      35 +  details: Record<string, unknown>; // rule-specific extras par
         +sed from the text (e.g. property name, old/new type, param name
         +)                                                              
      36 +  evidenceBefore: unknown | null;   // the JSON at jsonPointer

      37 +  evidenceAfter: unknown | null;    // same in the AFTER spec  
      38 +}                                                              
      39 +```

  ⎿  Created apps/server/drizzle/0001_append_only_triggers.sql (+91 -0)
       1 +-- Append-only protection (FR-39), enforced by the database its
         +elf.                                                           
       2 +-- Hand-written: drizzle-kit cannot express triggers.          
       3 +--                                                             
       4 +--   attempts, run_stage_results, call_sites, coverage_entries,
         + spec_versions:                                                
       5 +--       no UPDATE, no DELETE, no TRUNCATE.                    
       6 +--   runs:    no DELETE / TRUNCATE; no UPDATE once the OLD row 
         +is finished (finished_at IS NOT NULL).                         
       7 +--            Reanalysis = a new run with parent_run_id.       
       8 +--   changes: no DELETE / TRUNCATE; UPDATE may only touch the h
         +uman-confirmation columns                                      
       9 +--            mapping_status, mapping_source, mapping, confirme
         +d_by, confirmed_at.                                            
      10 +--                                                             
      11 +-- Errors use SQLSTATE 'P0001' (raise_exception) and start with
         + "append-only:".                                               
      12 +                                                               
      13 +CREATE FUNCTION sma_block_modification() RETURNS trigger       
      14 +LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$                                         
      15 +BEGIN                                                          
      16 +  RAISE EXCEPTION 'append-only: % on table "%" is not allowed (
         +FR-39)', TG_OP, TG_TABLE_NAME                                  
      17 +    USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';                                   
      18 +END;                                                           
      19 +$$;                                                            
      20 +--> statement-breakpoint                                       
      21 +CREATE FUNCTION sma_runs_guard_update() RETURNS trigger        
      22 +LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$                                         
      23 +BEGIN                                                          
      24 +  IF OLD.finished_at IS NOT NULL THEN                          
      25 +    RAISE EXCEPTION 'append-only: run % is finished and cannot 
         +be updated (FR-39); start a reanalysis run with parent_run_id i
         +nstead', OLD.id                                                
      26 +      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';                                 
      27 +  END IF;                                                      
      28 +  RETURN NEW;                                                  
      29 +END;                                                           
      30 +$$;                                                            
      31 +--> statement-breakpoint                                       
      32 +CREATE FUNCTION sma_changes_guard_update() RETURNS trigger     
      33 +LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$                                         
      34 +DECLARE                                                        
      35 +  mutable text[] := ARRAY['mapping_status', 'mapping_source', '
         +mapping', 'confirmed_by', 'confirmed_at'];                     
      36 +BEGIN                                                          
      37 +  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - mutable) IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) 
         +- mutable) THEN                                                
      38 +    RAISE EXCEPTION 'append-only: change % may only update mapp
         +ing confirmation columns (FR-39)', OLD.id                      
      39 +      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';                                 
      40 +  END IF;

       1 +import { drizzle, type NodePgDatabase } from 'drizzle-orm/node-
         +postgres';                                                     
       2 +import pg from 'pg';                                           
       3 +import * as schema from './schema.js';                         
       4 +                                                               
       5 +/** Typed Drizzle database handle used by every repository modu
         +le. */                                                         
       6 +export type Db = NodePgDatabase<typeof schema>;                
       7 +                                                               
       8 +/** A database handle together with the pool that backs it. */ 
       9 +export interface DbClient {                                    
      10 +  db: Db;                                                      
      11 +  pool: pg.Pool;                                               
      12 +  /** Closes every pooled connection. */                       
      13 +  close(): Promise<void>;                                      
      14 +}                                                              
      15 +                                                               
      16 +/**                                                            
      17 + * Creates a pooled PostgreSQL client. The connection string is
         + never logged (NFR-04).                                        
      18 + *                                                             
      19 + * @param connectionString - Postgres URL; defaults to `process
         +.env.DATABASE_URL`.                                            
      20 + * @throws Error when no connection string is available.       
      21 + */                                                            
      22 +export function createDbClient(connectionString = process.env.D
         +ATABASE_URL): DbClient {                                       
      23 +  if (!connectionString) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is not s
         +et');                                                          
      24 +  const pool = new pg.Pool({ connectionString, max: 10 });     
      25 +  const db = drizzle(pool, { schema });                        
      26 +  return { db, pool, close: () => pool.end() };                
      27 +}

       1 +# Decisions — Increment 03: Database schema                    
       2 +                                                               
       3 +| Date | Increment | Decision | Why |                          
       4 +| ---- | --------- | -------- | --- |                          
       5 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | `docs/SRS.pdf` is really a Word (.docx) fil
         +e with a `.pdf` extension. I read it by extracting `word/docume
         +nt.xml`. | Needed to read SRS 2.4, 3.9, DR-01–05, FR-39 for thi
         +s increment. The file was left unchanged (see report). |       
       6 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Added a `StageResultStatus` enum (`ok`/`fai
         +led`/`skipped`) to core, alongside the 13 listed enums. | `run_
         +stage_results.status` needs a pgEnum, and every pgEnum is built
         + from a core array. |                                          
       7 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | `evaluation_results.outcome` uses the `run_
         +status` enum. | An evaluation outcome is the final status of th
         +e scenario's run (repaired, flagged, failed, …). One vocabulary
         + for runs and results. |                                       
       8 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Primary keys are `uuid` with `gen_random_uu
         +id()`; money is `numeric(12,6)` (read back as a string); `confi
         +dence` is `double precision`; all timestamps are `timestamptz`.
         + | UUIDs are safe to expose in exports and URLs. numeric avoids
         + float rounding for cost; 6 decimals covers per-token prices. |
       9 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Every CHECK and UNIQUE constraint has an ex
         +plicit name, and the tests assert the exact constraint name tha
         +t fired. | Proves the intended rule rejected the row, not some 
         +other constraint. |                                            
      10 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Extra DB constraints beyond the prompt, eac
         +h with a rejection test: `credential_ref` must look like an env
         + var name (`^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*$`); `content_sha256` and `commit_
         +sha` formats; `status = 'running'` ⇔ `finished_at IS NULL`; `fi
         +nished_at ≥ started_at`; non-negative tokens, cost and duration
         +s; `tests_passed ≤ tests_total`; line/column ≥ 1; change `from 
         +≠ to` version; a run is not its own parent; `evaluation_results
         +.attempts_used` 0–4. | Moves more SRS rules into the database (
         +NFR-04: no secret can be stored as a credential reference). The
         + running ⇔ unfinished rule makes "finished" (and therefore froz
         +en, FR-39) unambiguous. |                                      
      11 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | The append-only triggers also block **TRUNC
         +ATE** on the protected tables (`BEFORE TRUNCATE … FOR EACH STAT
         +EMENT`). | Row-level triggers do not fire on TRUNCATE, which wo
         +uld otherwise bypass FR-39. |                                  
      12 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | The `changes` update trigger compares `to_j
         +sonb(NEW)` and `to_jsonb(OLD)` with the five confirmation colum
         +ns removed. `migration_note_id` is **not** updatable (exactly a
         +s the prompt lists). | One generic check covers every current a
         +nd future column. If Classify (06) needs to link a note after i
         +nsert, that becomes a deliberate migration. |                  
      13 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Trigger errors use SQLSTATE `P0001` and mes
         +sages starting with `append-only:`. | Lets later increments rec
         +ognize and report these errors consistently. |                 
      14 +| 2026-11-02 | 03 | `finishRun` defaults `finished_at` to the d
         +atabase's `now()`, not the host clock. | The Docker VM clock ca
         +n differ from the host's; a host timestamp failed the `finished
         +_at ≥ started_at` check in testing. |

         -status` enum. | An evaluation outcome is the final status of th
         -e scenario's run (repaired, flagged, failed, …). One vocabulary
         - for runs and results. |                                       
       8 -| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Primary keys are `uuid` with `gen_random_uu
         -id()`; money is `numeric(12,6)` (read back as a string); `confi
         -dence` is `double precision`; all timestamps are `timestamptz`.
         - | UUIDs are safe to expose in exports and URLs. numeric avoids
         - float rounding for cost; 6 decimals covers per-token prices. |
       9 -| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Every CHECK and UNIQUE constraint has an ex
         -plicit name, and the tests assert the exact constraint name tha
         -t fired. | Proves the intended rule rejected the row, not some 
         -other constraint. |                                            
      10 -| 2026-11-02 | 03 | Extra DB constraints beyond the prompt, eac
         -h with a rejection test: `credential_ref` must look like an env
         - var name (`^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*$`); `content_sha256` and `commit_
         -sha` formats; `status = 'running'` ⇔ `finished_at IS NULL`; `fi
         -nished_at ≥ started_at`; non-negative tokens, cost and duration
         -s; `tests_passed ≤ tests_total`; line/column ≥ 1; change `from 
         -≠ to` version; a run is not its own parent; `evaluation_results
         -.attempts_used` 0–4. | Moves more SRS rules into the database (
         -NFR-04: no secret can be stored as a credential reference). The
         - running ⇔ unfinished rule makes "finished" (and therefore froz
         -en, FR-39) unambiguous. |

         -ATE** on the protected tables (`BEFORE TRUNCATE … FOR EACH STAT
         -EMENT`). | Row-level triggers do not fire on TRUNCATE, which wo
         -uld otherwise bypass FR-39. |

         + `from ≠ to` version; a run is not its own parent; `evaluation_
         +results.attempts_used` 0–4. | Moves more SRS rules into the dat
         +abase (NFR-04: no secret can be stored as a credential referenc
         +e). The running ⇔ unfinished rule makes "finished" (and therefo
         +re frozen, FR-39) unambiguous. |                               
      11 +| 2026-11-02 | 03        | The append-only triggers also block 
         +**TRUNCATE** on the protected tables (`BEFORE TRUNCATE … FOR EA
         +CH STATEMENT`).                                                
         +                                                               
         +                                                               
         +                                                               
         +                                                               
         +                            | Row-level triggers do not fire on
         + TRUNCATE, which would otherwise bypass FR-39.                 
         +                                                               
         +                               |                               
      12 +| 2026-11-02 | 03        | The `changes` update trigger compare
         +s `to_jsonb(NEW)` and `to_jsonb(OLD)` with the five confirmatio
         +n columns removed. `migration_note_id` is **not** updatable (ex
         +actly as the prompt lists).                                    
         +                                                               
         +                                                               
         +                                                               
         +                            | One generic check covers every cu
         +rrent and future column. If Classify (06) needs to link a note 
         +after insert, that becomes a deliberate migration.

         +om the host's; a host timestamp failed the `finished_at ≥ start
         +ed_at` check in testing.

      15 +| 2026-11-02 | 03        | Tests derive their URL from `DATABAS

         + otherwise the code refuses. The Vitest `globalSetup` drops, re

         + run.

⏺ The team workflow is set up and in PR #15, but branch protection isn't on:
  GitHub refused it for this private repo. I didn't merge the PR. CI passes and

  PR: https://github.com/Bigyan1313/self_maintaining_api/pull/15

  What changed
  - Working rules: I rewrote Section 7 of the master context and copied it
    identically into CLAUDE.md. The diff confirms nothing else in the master
    context changed. The new rules:
    - start each increment by pulling main and confirming the issue is assigned
      to you
    - work on a feat/incNN-* branch, merge origin/main into it before opening
      the PR, and open a PR instead of merging yourself
    - a teammate merges it with a merge commit
    - one decisions file per increment
    - schema changes only as new migrations
    - packages/core only gains new files or exports
    - the chat log goes into the PR
  - Decisions: the 47 existing entries now live in docs/decisions/inc01.md,
    inc02.md and inc03.md; I checked the text is identical after the move.
    Today's choices are in inc03b.md, and docs/DECISIONS.md is now an index.
  - Labels: ready (green), blocked (red), critical (orange), increment (blue).
  - Unblock workflow: .github/workflows/unblock.yml uses actions/github-script
    v9.0.0, pinned to its full commit SHA. GitHub only runs it from main, so it
    starts working once PR #15 is merged; it hasn't been exercised yet.
  - TEAM.md: written in plain words. docs/PROGRESS.md gained an Issue column.
  - SRS rename: docs/SRS.pdf is now docs/SRS.docx (done with git mv). Links are
    updated in README.md, prompts/00 and prompts/01. Old chat logs and past
    decision entries still say SRS.pdf, as you asked.
